\noindent Type: \texttt{Outcome}.\par
\noindent Definition: \texttt{(match result with | Outcome.value computed steps => Outcome.value (computed) ((steps + extra)) | Outcome.overflow steps => Outcome.overflow ((steps + extra)) | Outcome.stuck => Outcome.stuck | Outcome.exhausted => Outcome.exhausted)}.\par
\noindent Axiom policy: \texttt{none}.\par

\noindent Type: \texttt{Option (LexLeanTarget.TargetSyntax.Value)}.\par

\noindent Axiom policy: \texttt{none}.\par
